{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-5-5","id":"msg_011CgesgspKytvNza4AoyWng","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017itEFV66wnPrFNwfr7xdhM","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/6f58665e8653/workspace/target/src/transport/conversationList.acceptance.test.ts","content":"import type {AddressInfo} from 'node:net';\nimport {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport {InMemorySessionStore} from '../infrastructure/stores/inMemorySessionStore.js';\nimport {setLogger} from '../shared/logger.js';\nimport {startEngineWith, stopEngines, connectClient} from './wsTestSupport.js';\n\n// Acceptance for #144: a client asks over the socket which conversations its user has, and is answered from the\n// connection's own user. Real upgrade, real socket, the engine's own store; nothing here is a double.\n\ninterface Frame {\n  type: string;\n  code?: string;\n  conversations?: Array<{sessionId: string; createdAt: unknown; updatedAt: unknown; entryCount: number}>;\n}\n\n// Spread in rather than written as a literal: the `history` option is the one this ticket adds, so it is not yet on\n// HalServerOptions, and a spread is the form that reaches the engine without a type error standing in for the red.\nconst historyOn = {history: {enabled: true}};\n\nconst at = (second: number): string => new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 0, 1, 0, 0, second)).toISOString();\n\n// Each conversation's activity is its newest entry, so the three are ordered by when the user last spoke in them.\nconst seedStore = (): InMemorySessionStore => {\n  const store = new InMemorySessionStore();\n  store.create('conv-a', 'u1').entries.push({role: 'user', content: 'a', timestamp: at(1)});\n  store.create('conv-b', 'u1').entries.push({role: 'user', content: 'b', timestamp: at(2)});\n  store.create('conv-c', 'u1').entries.push({role: 'user', content: 'c', timestamp: at(3)});\n  store.create('someone-elses', 'u2').entries.push({role: 'user', content: 'x', timestamp: at(4)});\n  return store;\n};\n\n// Sends one frame after the connected greeting, and resolves with the first answer that is either a list or an error.\nconst askForList = (url: string, frame: unknown): Promise<Frame> => {\n  const client = connectClient(url);\n  return new Promise<Frame>((resolve, reject) => {\n    const timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('no list or error answer within 2s')), 2000);\n    client.on('message', raw => {\n      const message = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as Frame;\n      if (message.type === 'connected') client.send(JSON.stringify(frame));\n      if (message.type === 'conversation_list' || message.type === 'error') {\n        clearTimeout(timer);\n        resolve(message);\n      }\n    });\n  });\n};\n\nbeforeAll(() => {\n  setLogger({debug: () => undefined, info: () => undefined, warn: () => undefined, error: () => undefined});\n});\n\nafterAll(() => {\n  setLogger();\n});\n\nafterEach(async () => {\n  await stopEngines();\n});\n\ndescribe('a client lists its own conversations over the socket', () => {\n  it('is answered with one conversation_list of its user’s conversations, newest first, with ISO times', async () => {\n    const {url} = await startEngineWith({sessionStore: seedStore(), ...historyOn});\n\n    const answer = await askForList(url, {type: 'list_conversations'});\n\n    expect(answer.type).toBe('conversation_list');\n    expect(answer.conversations?.map(row => row.sessionId)).toEqual(['conv-c', 'conv-b', 'conv-a']);\n    expect(\n      answer.conversations?.every(\n        row =>\n          typeof row.createdAt === 'string' &&\n          typeof row.updatedAt === 'string' &&\n          new Date(row.createdAt).toISOString() === row.createdAt &&\n          new Date(row.updatedAt as string).toISOString() === row.updatedAt\n      )\n    ).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it('is answered with the connecting user’s list even when the frame names another user', async () => {\n    const {url} = await startEngineWith({sessionStore: seedStore(), ...historyOn});\n\n    const answer = await askForList(url, {type: 'list_conversations', userId: 'u2'});\n\n    expect(answer.type).toBe('conversation_list');\n    expect(answer.conversations?.map(row => row.sessionId)).toEqual(['conv-c', 'conv-b', 'conv-a']);\n  });\n});\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3919,"cache_read_input_tokens":75187,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3919},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5142619a-9c5c-499a-9916-0832e59ad2b2","uuid":"5423cdae-9a19-4a5b-97d1-b6882aa0682a","timestamp":"2026-11-02T22:16:41.289Z","request_id":"req_011CgesgsbSBcWuxABFzxVgy"}
